<h2 class="display-3">Why Choose Edraw?</h2>
<p class="font-size-super mb-5">Professional, Trustworthy, Affordable</p>
<div class="row text-lg-left justify-content-center d-none d-lg-flex">
<div class="col-lg-3">
<div class="hover-shadow py-5 px-4 position-relative h-100">
<div class="font-size-super font-weight-bold mb-4">Mind Map Software</div>
<p class="font-size-small font-weight-light mb-5">"Wrapped in a comfortable-looking interface, Edraw Max brings rich features and tools for mind maps, like the diagram theme options and lots of exporting choices. Furthermore, some of the features, such as the Pencil or Pen tools to draw your own icons, are quite competitive compared to the majority of other free mind mapping tools."</p>
